    function getInitials(name?: string | null): string {
      if (!name) return '??';
      const parts = name.trim().split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean);
      if (parts.length === 0) return '??';
      if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0].slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
      return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
    }·
    function formatRecentGameDate(isoString: string): string {
      try {
        const d = new Date(isoString);
        if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
        const weekday = d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short' });
        const day = d.getDate();
        const month = d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short' });
        const time = d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
        return `${weekday} ${day} ${month}, ${time}`;
      } catch {
        return '';
      }
    }·
    const GROUP_BADGE_COLORS = [
      colors.primary,
      colors.success,
      colors.warning,
      colors.accentPurple,
    ];·
    const LEVEL_LABEL_KEY: Record<SkillLevelValue, LabelKey> = {
      beginner: 'skill_level.beginner',
      intermediate: 'skill_level.intermediate',
      expert: 'skill_level.expert',
    };·
    function getLevelTextColor(level?: string | null): string {
      const normalized = level?.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (normalized === 'beginner') return colors.warning;
      if (normalized === 'intermediate') return colors.primary;
      if (normalized === 'expert') return colors.accentPurple;
      return colors.textPrimary;
    }·
    function getLevelBgColor(level?: string | null): string {
      const normalized = level?.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (normalized === 'beginner') return colors.warningLight;
      if (normalized === 'intermediate') return colors.primaryLight;
      if (normalized === 'expert') return colors.avatarTint5;
      return colors.avatarTint1;
    }·
    function SkillTileItem({
      sport,
      level,
    }: {
      sport: string;
      level: string;
    }): React.JSX.Element {
      const t = useT();
      const sportName = useSportDisplayName(sport);
      const visual = useSportVisual(sport);
      const palette = sportPalette(visual.color);
      const textColor = getLevelTextColor(level);
      const bgColor = getLevelBgColor(level);
      const normalized = level.trim().toLowerCase() as SkillLevelValue;
      const labelKey = LEVEL_LABEL_KEY[normalized];
      const levelText = labelKey ? t(labelKey) : level;·
      return (
        <View style={[styles.skillTile, { backgroundColor: bgColor }]}>
          <View style={[styles.skillIconCircle, { backgroundColor: palette.chip }]}>
            <SportIcon iconKey={visual.iconKey} size={20} />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.skillTextCol}>
            <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.skillSportName}>
              {sportName}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.skillLevelText, { color: textColor }]}>
              {levelText}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      );
    }·
    function GameSportMark({ sport }: { sport: string }): React.JSX.Element {
      const visual = useSportVisual(sport);
      const palette = sportPalette(visual.color);
      return (
        <View style={[styles.gameSportIconCircle, { backgroundColor: palette.chip }]}>
          <SportIcon iconKey={visual.iconKey} size={20} />
        </View>
      );
    }·

      const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

      // ── Perf: render count and screen timing ──────────────────────────────────
      usePerfRender('ProfileScreen');
      usePerfScreen('ProfileScreen', isLoading);·
      // ── Perf: 7-tap diagnostics toggle (Item SWITCH) ──────────────────────────
      // Tracks taps on the display name within a 3-second window.
      const tapTimestamps = useRef<number[]>([]);·
      const handleDisplayNamePress = useCallback((): void => {
        const now = Date.now();
        // Keep only taps within the last 3000 ms.
        tapTimestamps.current = tapTimestamps.current.filter(ts => now - ts < 3000);
        tapTimestamps.current.push(now);
        if (tapTimestamps.current.length >= 7) {
          tapTimestamps.current = [];
          const enabled = isPerfEnabled();
          const buttons: AlertButton[] = [
            {
              text: enabled ? 'Turn OFF' : 'Turn ON',
              onPress: () => {
                setRuntimeFlag(!enabled).catch(() => {});
              },
            },
            {
              text: 'Close',
              style: 'cancel',
            },
          ];
          if (enabled) {
            buttons.splice(1, 0, {
              text: 'Share log',
              onPress: () => {
                shareLog().catch(() => {});
              },
            });
          }
          Alert.alert(t(enabled ? 'mob.diagnostics.title_on' : 'mob.diagnostics.title_off'), '', buttons);
        }
      }, [t]);·
      const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
          setLoadError(null);
          // ── Perf: instrument each parallel call with a span ────────────────
          const profileSpan = startSpan('fetch', { resource: 'getProfile', screen: 'ProfileScreen' });
          const summarySpan = startSpan('fetch', { resource: 'getProfileSummary', screen: 'ProfileScreen' });
          const groupsSpan = startSpan('fetch', { resource: 'getMyGroups', screen: 'ProfileScreen' });
          const bannersSpan = startSpan('fetch', { resource: 'getCachedBanners', screen: 'ProfileScreen' });·
          const [profileData, summaryData, myGroupsData, bannerData] = await Promise.all([
            getProfile().then(r => { profileSpan({ result: 'ok' }); return r; }).catch(e => { profileSpan({ result: 'error' }); throw e; }),
            getProfileSummary().then(r => { summarySpan({ result: 'ok' }); return r; }).catch(() => {
              summarySpan({ result: 'fallback' });
              return { games_played: 0, games_hosted: 0, groups_count: 0, recent_games: [] };
            }),
            getMyGroups().then(r => { groupsSpan({ result: 'ok' }); return r; }).catch(() => {
              groupsSpan({ result: 'fallback' });
              return { items: [], total: 0 };
            }),
            getCachedBanners().then(r => { bannersSpan({ result: 'ok' }); return r; }).catch(() => {
              bannersSpan({ result: 'fallback' });
              return null;
            }),
          ]);
          setProfile(profileData);
          setProfileSummary(summaryData);
          setGroups(myGroupsData.items ?? []);
          if (bannerData?.fallback) {
            setBannerFallback(bannerData.fallback);
          }
        } catch {
          setLoadError(t('mob.profile.load_error'));
        } finally {
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }, [t]);·
      useFocusEffect(
        useCallback(() => {
          loadData();

    function getInitials(name?: string | null): string {
      if (!name) return '??';
      const parts = name.trim().split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean);
      if (parts.length === 0) return '??';
      if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0].slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
      return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
    }·
    function formatRecentGameDate(isoString: string): string {
      try {
        const d = new Date(isoString);
        if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return '';
        const weekday = d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short' });
        const day = d.getDate();
        const month = d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short' });
        const time = d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
        return `${weekday} ${day} ${month}, ${time}`;
      } catch {
        return '';
      }
    }·
    const GROUP_BADGE_COLORS = [
      colors.primary,
      colors.success,
      colors.warning,
      colors.accentPurple,
    ];·
    const LEVEL_LABEL_KEY: Record<SkillLevelValue, LabelKey> = {
      beginner: 'skill_level.beginner',
      intermediate: 'skill_level.intermediate',
      expert: 'skill_level.expert',
    };·
    function getLevelTextColor(level?: string | null): string {
      const normalized = level?.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (normalized === 'beginner') return colors.warning;
      if (normalized === 'intermediate') return colors.primary;
      if (normalized === 'expert') return colors.accentPurple;
      return colors.textPrimary;
    }·
    function getLevelBgColor(level?: string | null): string {
      const normalized = level?.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (normalized === 'beginner') return colors.warningLight;
      if (normalized === 'intermediate') return colors.primaryLight;
      if (normalized === 'expert') return colors.avatarTint5;
      return colors.avatarTint1;
    }·
    function SkillTileItem({
      sport,
      level,
    }: {
      sport: string;
      level: string;
    }): React.JSX.Element {
      const t = useT();
      const sportName = useSportDisplayName(sport);
      const visual = useSportVisual(sport);
      const palette = sportPalette(visual.color);
      const textColor = getLevelTextColor(level);
      const bgColor = getLevelBgColor(level);
      const normalized = level.trim().toLowerCase() as SkillLevelValue;
      const labelKey = LEVEL_LABEL_KEY[normalized];
      const levelText = labelKey ? t(labelKey) : level;·
      return (
        <View style={[styles.skillTile, { backgroundColor: bgColor }]}>
          <View style={[styles.skillIconCircle, { backgroundColor: palette.chip }]}>
            <SportIcon iconKey={visual.iconKey} size={20} />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.skillTextCol}>
            <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.skillSportName}>
              {sportName}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.skillLevelText, { color: textColor }]}>
              {levelText}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </View>
      );
    }·
    function GameSportMark({ sport }: { sport: string }): React.JSX.Element {
      const visual = useSportVisual(sport);
      const palette = sportPalette(visual.color);
      return (
        <View style={[styles.gameSportIconCircle, { backgroundColor: palette.chip }]}>
          <SportIcon iconKey={visual.iconKey} size={20} />
        </View>
      );
    }·
    export default function ProfileScreen({ navigation }: Props): React.JSX.Element {
      const { signOut } = useAuth();
      const t = useT();·
      const [profile, setProfile] = useState<UserProfile | null>(null);
      const [profileSummary, setProfileSummary] = useState<ProfileSummary | null>(null);
      const [groups, setGroups] = useState<Group[]>([]);
      const [bannerFallback, setBannerFallback] = useState<BannerFallback | null>(null);
      const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
      const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);
      const [isSigningOut, setIsSigningOut] = useState(false);·
      // ── Perf: render count and screen timing ──────────────────────────────────
      usePerfRender('ProfileScreen');
      usePerfScreen('ProfileScreen', isLoading);·
      // ── Perf: 7-tap diagnostics toggle (Item SWITCH) ──────────────────────────
      // Tracks taps on the display name within a 3-second window.
      const tapTimestamps = useRef<number[]>([]);·
      const handleDisplayNamePress = useCallback((): void => {
        const now = Date.now();
        // Keep only taps within the last 3000 ms.
        tapTimestamps.current = tapTimestamps.current.filter(ts => now - ts < 3000);
        tapTimestamps.current.push(now);
        if (tapTimestamps.current.length >= 7) {
          tapTimestamps.current = [];
          const enabled = isPerfEnabled();
          const buttons: AlertButton[] = [
            {
              text: enabled ? 'Turn OFF' : 'Turn ON',
              onPress: () => {
                setRuntimeFlag(!enabled).catch(() => {});
              },
            },
            {
              text: 'Close',
              style: 'cancel',
            },
          ];
          if (enabled) {
            buttons.splice(1, 0, {
              text: 'Share log',
              onPress: () => {
                shareLog().catch(() => {});
              },
            });
          }
          Alert.alert(t(enabled ? 'mob.diagnostics.title_on' : 'mob.diagnostics.title_off'), '', buttons);
        }
      }, [t]);·
      const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
          setLoadError(null);
          // ── Perf: instrument each parallel call with a span ────────────────
          const profileSpan = startSpan('fetch', { resource: 'getProfile', screen: 'ProfileScreen' });
          const summarySpan = startSpan('fetch', { resource: 'getProfileSummary', screen: 'ProfileScreen' });
          const groupsSpan = startSpan('fetch', { resource: 'getMyGroups', screen: 'ProfileScreen' });
          const bannersSpan = startSpan('fetch', { resource: 'getCachedBanners', screen: 'ProfileScreen' });·
          const [profileData, summaryData, myGroupsData, bannerData] = await Promise.all([
            getProfile().then(r => { profileSpan({ result: 'ok' }); return r; }).catch(e => { profileSpan({ result: 'error' }); throw e; }),
            getProfileSummary().then(r => { summarySpan({ result: 'ok' }); return r; }).catch(() => {
              summarySpan({ result: 'fallback' });
              return { games_played: 0, games_hosted: 0, groups_count: 0, recent_games: [] };
            }),
            getMyGroups().then(r => { groupsSpan({ result: 'ok' }); return r; }).catch(() => {
              groupsSpan({ result: 'fallback' });
              return { items: [], total: 0 };
            }),
            getCachedBanners().then(r => { bannersSpan({ result: 'ok' }); return r; }).catch(() => {
              bannersSpan({ result: 'fallback' });
              return null;
            }),
          ]);
          setProfile(profileData);
          setProfileSummary(summaryData);
          setGroups(myGroupsData.items ?? []);
          if (bannerData?.fallback) {
            setBannerFallback(bannerData.fallback);
          }
        } catch {
          setLoadError(t('mob.profile.load_error'));
        } finally {
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }, [t]);·
      useFocusEffect(
        useCallback(() => {
          loadData();
        }, [loadData]),
      );·
      const handleSignOut = async (): Promise<void> => {
